831 entries, Codyssey to cognitive-loop-map. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **Codyssey**: 이 Skill은 Codyssey Missions를 수행할 때 반복되는 준비, 구현 지원, 검증, Evidence 정리, 평가기준 대조, 설명 준비, 완료 판정 작업을 일관되고 안전하게 수행하기 위한 실행 가이드다. ([hauteville1862/codyssey-b1-1](https://github.com/hauteville1862/codyssey-b1-1/tree/HEAD/Codyssey))
- **codyssey-mission**: Codyssey 1-1 미션(리눅스 CLI·파일 권한·Docker 이미지/컨테이너/포트/볼륨·Git 연동)의 평가 항목 1~5를 5단계 순서(원리 공부 → 구조 설계 → 실습·실패 실험 → 심층 인터뷰 PREP 카드 → README 조립)로 공부하며 문서화하도록 돕는 스킬. - **coefficient-and-standard-error-interpretation**: Use when after fitting a linear model to expression data using limma's lmFit function on a design matrix encoding experimental groups, inspect the resulting MArrayLM object to retrieve coefficient estimates (log-fold-changes) and standard errors needed to assess which genes show meaningful. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/coefficient-and-standard-error-interpretation))
- **coefficient-of-determination-model-assessment**: Use when a fitted model (e.g. radar fixed-effects NMDS1 models) must be validated on independent external plots, and predicted-vs-observed agreement needs to be quantified as R² per taxon. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/coefficient-of-determination-model-assessment))
- **coefficient-of-variation-analysis**: Use when two peak-filling strategies (e.g., XCMS default fillPeaks vs. ncGTW-realigned fillPeaks) must be compared on feature intensity consistency, to verify that realignment reduces CV for detected misaligned features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-analysis))
- **coefficient-of-variation-calculation-and-filtering**: Use when after loading and applying minimum non-missing-value thresholds to an omics expression dataset (proteomics, metabolomics, or other panomics data). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-calculation-and-filtering))
- **coefficient-of-variation-comparison**: Use when after normalization of a metabolomic feature matrix but before statistical testing, when you have both QC (technical replicate) and non-QC (study) samples and need to remove features with unstable or poorly reproducible signal patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-comparison) · 2 copies)
- **coefficient-of-variation-computation**: Use when you have loaded raw NMR or MS metabolomic abundance data into a SummarizedExperiment object and need to assess feature reproducibility before downstream association modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-computation))
- **coefficient-of-variation-computation**: Use when you have repeated (e.g., 10×) timing measurements per method per chromosome and need a dimensionless stability metric to compare run-time consistency across methods or configurations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-computation))
- **coefficient-of-variation-computation-across-datasets**: Use when comparing peak-area reproducibility of an untargeted metabolomics peak list before vs. after a peak-curation or reintegration step, e.g. to verify that PeakDetective reintegration of artifact peaks lowers the CV of peak areas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-computation-across-datasets))
- **coefficient-of-variation-estimation**: Use when you have ≥3 raw mass-spectrometry samples (e.g. .mzML/.mzXML/.CDF) and need to quantify how stable AutoTuner-derived peak-picking parameter estimates are as sample count grows, to pick a minimum sample size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-estimation--39931a706c2e8dfe))
- **coefficient-of-variation-estimation**: Use when replicate (blind duplicate) measurements are available for NMR metabolic biomarker data and you need to quantify and compare measurement reproducibility (CV%, R2) before and after a quality control or technical-variation-removal procedure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-estimation--dff39758e3e501ad))
- **coefficient-of-variation-inflation-quantification**: Use when a candidate batch-correction output on multi-batch metabolomics data must be checked for variance inflation: compute per-feature VCs before and after normalization and the fraction of features whose VC increased by &gt;=5% (allowed_vc_increase). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-inflation-quantification))
- **coefficient-of-variation-precision-computation**: Use when benchmarking transcript quantification tools on spike-in DRS data with an equal-concentration mix (e.g. SIRV E0 from SRR6058583), where lower variability in measured isoform abundances indicates better quantification precision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-precision-computation))
- **coefficient-of-variation-qc-assessment**: Use when a multi-batch metabolomics feature-count matrix has been normalized (e.g. by RALPS) and you need QC metrics — batch-wise VC and replicate cross-correlation — to judge whether batch effects were reduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-qc-assessment))
- **coefficient-of-variation-ratio-filtering-samples-vs-qc**: Use when a feature abundance table from MS-DIAL (with a sample annotation file labeling QC vs biological samples) needs quality filtering based on the ratio of coefficient of variation across samples vs QCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-ratio-filtering-samples-vs-qc))
- **coefficient-of-variation-threshold-analysis**: Use when you have per-feature CV values from quality control analysis of NMR or MS metabolomic data and need to: (1) establish whether your dataset meets FDA reproducibility standards for downstream biomarker discovery or quantification; (2) benchmark data quality against regulatory thresholds; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-threshold-analysis))
- **coefficient-of-variation-thresholding**: Use when a metabonomic feature matrix is accompanied by a per-feature CV table and non-reproducible spectral features must be removed before downstream MWAS analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-thresholding))
- **coefficient-of-variation-variance-stabilization-analysis**: Use when evaluating whether an imputation method reduces gene-expression variability across cells of the same type — i.e., you have unimputed and imputed count/log matrices plus cell-type annotations and need a variance-stabilization comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/coefficient-of-variation-variance-stabilization-analysis))
- **coefficient-table-interpretation**: Use when you have per-donor flow cytometry cell proportion data plus donor metadata (age, sex) and need to determine which individual-level factor is most strongly associated with each immune cell population's composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/coefficient-table-interpretation))
- **coeluted-peak-deconvolution**: Use when processing LC-MS/MS MRM chromatograms (mzML or vendor-converted raw data) where targeted compounds have coeluting isomers or neighbors on one side of the peak, and accurate per-isomer quantitation requires splitting those overlapping peaks before regression or abundance normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coeluted-peak-deconvolution))
- **coeluted-peak-fitting**: Use when an MRM ion chromatogram contains overlapping/coeluted peaks for a targeted compound (e.g., isomers sharing a transition, like the PC 34:3 isomers at RTs 4.72, 4.84, and 4.92 min) and per-peak abundances must be separated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coeluted-peak-fitting))
- **coeluting-compound-resolution**: Use when analyzing complex GC-MS mixtures where two or more chemical compounds elute at similar or identical retention times, producing overlapping or merged peaks in the raw chromatogram. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/coeluting-compound-resolution) · 4 copies)
- **coeluting-ion-correlation-grouping**: Use when you have IDSL.IPA-generated 'peaklists' and 'peak_alignment' directories plus MS1-level HRMS data and need to group co-eluting MS1 ions (isotopologues, adducts, in-source fragments) into composite spectra clusters for chemical annotation without MS2 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coeluting-ion-correlation-grouping))
- **coeluting-ion-separation**: Use when raw GC-MS data (netCDF or mzML format) exhibits overlapping chromatographic peaks—i.e., when multiple analytes elute at similar retention times and produce composite mass spectra with ambiguous m/z signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/coeluting-ion-separation) · 2 copies)
- **coeluting-isobaric-lipid-feature-analysis**: Use when an LC-MS/MS lipidomics peak table contains chromatographic features with multiple isobaric coeluting lipid spectral matches and you must quantify feature purity before assigning a confident identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/coeluting-isobaric-lipid-feature-analysis))
- **coenzyme-a-fractional-abundance-simulation**: Use when benchmarking isotope-correction methods (ElemCor MDT/ULS, original ULS, NRE, FAM) on simulated or measured FAM for large sulfur- or nitrogen-containing metabolites at high nominal instrument resolution, or when validating 34S/13C correction accuracy for CoA-pathway metabolites. - **coeval-audit**: Audit a developer's own agent skill with a Coeval instance — connect an approved first Check, capture real skill runs, submit them for judging, and report Results honestly. Use when the user says "audit my skill with coeval", "submit results to coeval", "capture skill runs", or "judge these outputs". For a new or unclear setup, begin with the sibling coeval-setup skill's context-first interview and approved proposal. Do not use it for human adjudication, Golden promotion, or release decisions; those stay in the Coeval dashboard. ([luka-zivkovic/coeval](https://github.com/luka-zivkovic/coeval/tree/HEAD/plugins/coeval/skills/coeval-audit))
- **coeval-setup**: Guide a beginner through setting up Coeval for an AI agent, workflow, prompt, or skill. Inspect safe project text, identify the target and recorded evidence, ask a short context-aware question, propose one plain-language Check, then connect and create it as Starter · unvalidated. - **cognitive-load-theory**: Sweller's Cognitive Load Theory (CLT) for agents writing skill content, designing prompts, building UI/dashboards, and authoring documentation. Working memory holds roughly 4 chunks at a time; CLT classifies load into three types — intrinsic (irreducible task difficulty), extraneous (unnecessary load from poor presentation, ELIMINATE), and germane (load that builds schemas, PROMOTE).
